00041:     assert radius > 0
00042:     assert all(sum((a[k]-b[k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-15
00043:                for a, b in zip(pts, pts[1:]))

00295: # Resolve compiled descendants by containment in registered semantic part
00296: # bounds, not by shared finish color or unreliable primitive object names.
00297: def mesh_bounds(obj):
00298:     pts = [obj.matrix_world @ Vector(v) for v in obj.bound_box]
00299:     return ([min(p[k] for p in pts) for k in range(3)],
00300:             [max(p[k] for p in pts) for k in range(3)])
00301: 
00302: def contained(lo,hi,region):
00303:     rlo,rhi = region
00304:     eps = .00015
00305:     return all(lo[k] >= rlo[k]-eps and hi[k] <= rhi[k]+eps
00306:                for k in range(3))
00307: 
00308: for obj in sc.objects:
00309:     if obj.type != 'MESH':
00310:         continue
00311:     lo,hi = mesh_bounds(obj)
00312:     chosen = None
00313:     if hi[2] < -.001:
00314:         chosen = floor_mat
00315:     elif any(contained(lo,hi,r) for r in fabric_regions):
00316:         chosen = fabric
00317:     if chosen is not None:
00318:         obj.data.materials.clear()
00319:         obj.data.materials.append(chosen)

00036: def cube(parent,key,size,center,color):
00037:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v>0 for v in size)
00038:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00039:     return parent.include(key,box(size=size,center=center,color=color))
00040: 
00041: def tube(parent,key,a,b,radius=.018,color=dark):
00042:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius>0
00043:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (*a,*b))
00044:     assert sum((a[i]-b[i])**2 for i in range(3))>0
00045:     return parent.include(key,rod(radius=radius,start=a,end=b,color=color))
00046: 
00047: def sweep(parent,key,points,radius,color):
00048:     assert radius>0 and math.isfinite(radius)
00049:     assert all(sum((a[i]-b[i])**2 for i in range(3))>0
00050:                for a,b in zip(points,points[1:]))
00051:     return parent.include(key,sweep_tube(points=points,radius=radius,color=color))

00005: # Inventory: cream compartment, red-brown deck, exposed ceiling beams and pipes,
00006: # three left berths, two louvered cabinets on a green bench, wall ventilator,
00007: # rear lockers, chair, chest, enclosure, painted column, board, fire station,
00008: # cropped right bench. Near: column and benches. Far: lockers and rear pipes.
00009: # Cage rails occlude the rear furniture. Dimensions are arbitrary-scale hypotheses.
00010: #
00011: # Revision family: geometry/placement of the port furnishings.
00012: # Visual observation: step 2 approximately aligns the column foot and enclosure,
00013: # while the port cabinets are cropped and the ventilator and berths extend too
00014: # far toward the camera. This suggests a local depth-placement error.
00015: # Affected modules: port cabinets, berths with all posts/rails, wall ventilator.
00016: # Move these complete assemblies rearward; lower the two upper berth surfaces
00017: # and their edge rails together. The existing bench supports the shifted cabinets.
00018: # Preserve camera, architecture, right-side anchors, rear objects and appearance.
00019: # Risk: inferred berth length/depth remains uncertain; farther placement reduces
00020: # apparent width. Runtime fact: steps 1 and 2 succeeded; this revision is untested.
00021:

00036: def cube(parent,key,size,center,color):
00037:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v>0 for v in size)
00038:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00039:     return parent.include(key,box(size=size,center=center,color=color))
00040: 
00041: def tube(parent,key,a,b,radius=.018,color=dark):
00042:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius>0
00043:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (*a,*b))
00044:     assert sum((a[i]-b[i])**2 for i in range(3))>0
00045:     return parent.include(key,rod(radius=radius,start=a,end=b,color=color))
00046: 
00047: def sweep(parent,key,points,radius,color):
00048:     assert radius>0 and math.isfinite(radius)
00049:     assert all(sum((a[i]-b[i])**2 for i in range(3))>0

00037:     n = ball(1, center=center, color=color, opacity=opacity)
00038:     n.resize(radii, pivot=center)

00044: def loop_points(center, r1, r2, plane="xz", count=24):
00045:     pts = []
00046:     for i in range(count + 1):
00047:         a = 2 * math.pi * i / count
00048:         if plane == "xz":
00049:             pts.append((center[0]+r1*math.cos(a), center[1],
00050:                         center[2]+r2*math.sin(a)))
00051:         else:
00052:             pts.append((center[0], center[1]+r1*math.cos(a),
00053:                         center[2]+r2*math.sin(a)))
00054:     return pts

00261: def material(name, color, roughness=.5, metallic=0):
00262:     m = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00263:     m.use_nodes = True
00264:     p = m.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00265:     p.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*color,1)
00266:     p.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00267:     p.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
00268:     return m
